### Uncompiled observed-repeated-program-slots.lean.txt

SHA256 8b1149d4824e3f1e2de6c9423395a168090863ccbd18155ce3c8f1f3e90d2398.

```text
-- Append after existing sections in the eager reservation namespace.
-- Use the runtime SelectedPrefix216 public local-law draft.
section ObservedRepeatedProgramSlots
open EnumerationCompressionFrame
open SourceSynthesisHereditaryProgramFields (ValueFields)
open SourceSynthesisProgramEnumerationRoles (ProgramSlots)
local notation "NativeHeap" => InternedConstructorTemplates.State Symbol Constraint
local notation "Core" => EnumerationState.State Symbol Constraint
local notation "literal" => SourceSynthesisLabelProgramBuildRoles.literal

private theorem observed_repeated_program_slots
    (heap : NativeHeap) (fuel uv handle : Nat) (scs : List Suspended)
    (before readAfter checked : Core) (safe : Safe before)
    (valid : Constraint → Prop) (marked : Nat → Prop)
    (slots : ProgramSlots valid literal heap before marked)
    (reading : Success (getValue fuel uv) before (.unenumerated (some handle) scs) readAfter)
    (checking : Success (SourcePruningEnumerationRecursion.checkRepeated heap fuel uv handle scs)
      readAfter true checked) : ProgramSlots valid literal heap checked marked := by
  obtain ⟨readFrame, oldRoot, size, oldPath, _readPath, oldStored⟩ :=
    getValue_frame fuel uv before readAfter _ safe.1.total reading
  cases decoded : lookupNode heap handle with
  | none =>
      have impossible : Success (failure .unresolvedReference) readAfter true checked := by
        simpa only [SourcePruningEnumerationRecursion.checkRepeated, decoded] using checking
      exact (success_failure .unresolvedReference readAfter checked true impossible).elim
  | some value =>
      by_cases boundary : MergedOrdinaryHeight.recursiveBoundary value = true
      · simp only [SourcePruningEnumerationRecursion.checkRepeated, decoded, boundary, ↓reduceIte] at checking
        obtain ⟨selected, resolved, resolution, continued⟩ := (success_bind _ _ _ _ _).mp checking
        have resolvedFrame := representative_frame fuel uv selected readAfter resolved
          (readFrame.total safe.1.total) resolution
        have wholeFrame := readFrame.trans resolvedFrame
        obtain ⟨found, lookup, selectedSame, _resolvedSame⟩ :=
          (representative_success fuel uv readAfter resolved selected).mp resolution
        have path := (UnionFind.findRoot_correct fuel readAfter.representatives uv found lookup).1
        have rootSame : selected = oldRoot := selectedSame.trans
          (UnionFind.reaches_unique path ((readFrame.reaches uv oldRoot size).mpr oldPath)).1
        have stored : resolved.values[selected]? = some (.unenumerated (some handle) scs) := by
          simpa only [wholeFrame.values, rootSame] using oldStored
        have resolvedSlots := SourceSynthesisProgramRolesCompression.compression_frame_program_slots
          heap before resolved marked wholeFrame slots
        obtain ⟨current, currentState, read, continued⟩ := (success_bind _ _ _ _ _).mp continued
        obtain ⟨currentSame, stateSame⟩ := (EnumerationDriver.get_success resolved currentState current).mp read
        subst current
        subst currentState
        by_cases seen : recursionKey handle scs ∈ resolved.recursionAncestry selected
        · rw [ite_eq_left seen] at continued
          obtain ⟨token, written, writing, finished⟩ := (success_bind _ _ _ _ _).mp continued
          cases token
          obtain ⟨_answer, afterSame⟩ := (success_pure true true written checked).mp finished
          rw [afterSame]
          apply SourceSynthesisProgramRolesSelectedPrefix.write_slots heap resolved written marked resolvedSlots selected
            (.unenumerated (some handle) []) ?_ ?_ writing
          · intro held tagged sc member
            cases member
          · intro tagged
            obtain ⟨payload, present, role⟩ := resolvedSlots.stored selected tagged
            have payloadSame : payload = .unenumerated (some handle) scs :=
              Option.some.inj (present.symm.trans stored)
            subst payload
            cases role with
            | pending native bucket original found fields _validTries _annotationPaths =>
                have same : original = value := Option.some.inj (found.symm.trans decoded)
                have valueFields : ValueFields valid literal value := same ▸ fields
                cases valueFields <;> cases boundary
        · rw [ite_eq_right seen] at continued
          obtain ⟨impossible, _same⟩ := (success_pure false true resolved checked).mp continued
          cases impossible
      · have impossible : Success (pure false : EnumerateM Symbol Constraint Bool) readAfter true checked := by
          simpa only [SourcePruningEnumerationRecursion.checkRepeated, decoded, ite_eq_right boundary] using checking
        obtain ⟨contradiction, _same⟩ := (success_pure false true readAfter checked).mp impossible
        cases contradiction

end ObservedRepeatedProgramSlots
```

    (oldHandle : Nat) (scs : List Suspended) (readAfter checked : Core) (oldValue value : Node)

## Small completed proof draft

This is a PRIVATE actual successful read/repeat-check observer, not a producer.
It uses the same native heap, actual readAfter and checked Core, old marks,
input Safe and current slots. Frame transport retains roots/stores/incoming.
The exact actual clear writes an empty bucket. The new public write_slots
closes that write. A marked cleared target would have current ValueFields,
which contradict recursiveBoundary=true. No mark, native caller or Memos changes.
The small proof follows the old checked repeated-branch argument and replaces
its private write helper with the new public local law. It adds no framework.
No new Eq instance is introduced. Append outside an instance-bearing section;
the fragment scopes its own aliases/opens and needs no Eq hypotheses.
Root must still strictly check this draft against the216-line visibility draft.

## Exact full contract and remaining narrowed-arm steps

private-observed-prefix-program-slots-header.txt:26 lines; SHA
01d2f7a21044e35bf210cdc400a5199fb7c24edd144641433d3c709020e8a1ae.
This is byte-identical to the frozen0515 contract. There is no proof placeholder
or admitted theorem in a Lean module. The exact top-level proof is still open.

Remaining steps at the SAME literal runPrefix observation:
1. Deconstruct existing PrefixValid, retaining its full Post/final Memos/query
   and the actual old/new native values/heaps/handles/caches. Do not infer full
   Memos equality from query equality. Do not run another native producer.
2. Convert actual getValue and false repeat checks to Frames. Apply public
   compression_frame_program_slots and keeps_program_slots at SAME heaps.
3. Extract the executed action's actual raw singleton from the same runPrefix
   action field. This is a source-operation observation, not a desired roles
   supplier. Its exact remaining19-line header has SHA
   937b66e6f5a66f1c0879d40c22854461e86a9a7b6a72950638dfd165f4091902
   in remaining-observed_prefix_action-header.txt.
4. Use public prepared_action to split the exact action into pure or the
   actual representative/write action. Read Frames retain literal raw marks.
5. For a marked write target, derive old ValueFields from current stored roles,
   use public narrowed_fields at actual native fuel, and derive actual result
   availability/closedness/readback. The exact remaining8-line header is SHA
   cf97b91b1657002cb16da2cb3bcdbfb195b6898c0aeea7ed8ebbb99b958c9e0b
   in remaining-observed_narrow_available-header.txt. Existing public
   narrowAt_edges/makeNode_available/makeNode_closed/available_readback support
   it, but there is no callable direct observer for arbitrary successful fuel.
6. Apply public write_slots. Derive its written-payload Role condition inside
   this proof. Do not expose it as a new whole-prefix caller requirement.
7. The literal .inr Right callback does not change Core. Reattach SAME cache,
   reached caller and old marks. Parked SOME uses the repeated observer above.
   Narrowed NONE has no returned child cache. It does not justify failed-state
   roles. PrefixValid excludes stopped/parked-NONE successful-prefix cases.

The old two extra helpers are59 and50 lines in SharedDriver1859. Copying those,
its prepared observer and the full90-line join would recreate a large stack.
This bounded draft therefore stops at the actual clear branch and freezes the
exact remaining observer interfaces. Root can review the smallest common
operation-observer placement before authorizing that implementation.

Own ordinary_prefix_packet is already handled by the SAME actual class
allocation law in checked4087. Initial selected-value preparation separately
retains Post/RenderStable/exact stores; it needs only native Keeps and pure
RenderStable role transport. Neither fact substitutes for the outer prefix.
All coupled prefix resource receipts remain intact. No input-model pullback,
model existence, partial-Type soundness or complete eager role claim is made.
No compiler, Git, live source, root docs, evidence, Haskell or board operation.
